**Bron:** `GTG/Kennis/strong/[rom,1co,2co,gal,eph,phi,col,1th,2th,1ti,2ti,tit,phm,heb].jsonl`

**Boeken in deze bundel:** Romeinen, 1 Korinthe, 2 Korinthe, Galaten, Efeze, Filippenzen, Kolossenzen, 1 Thessalonicenzen, 2 Thessalonicenzen, 1 Timotheus, 2 Timotheus, Titus, Filemon, Hebreeen
